\displaystyle\lesssim\sum^{L-1}_{k=0}(2^{k}\rho)^{\frac{-sq}{q-1}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}|u(y)-(u)_{\rho}|^{q-1}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}+\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{2^{L}\rho}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{2^{L}\rho}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}

\displaystyle\quad+(2^{L}\rho)^{\frac{-sq}{q-1}}\sum^{L-1}_{k=0}|(u)_{2^{k+1}\rho}-(u)_{2^{k}\rho}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum^{L-1}_{k=0}(2^{k}\rho)^{\frac{-sq}{q-1}}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}|u(y)-(u)_{\rho}|^{q-1}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}|u(y)-(u)_{2^{k+1}\rho}|^{q-1}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{2^{L}\rho}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{2^{L}\rho}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}.

(3.14)

Furthermore, we observe that

\displaystyle\sum^{L-1}_{k=0}(2^{k}\rho)^{\frac{-sq}{q-1}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}|u(y)-(u)_{\rho}|^{q-1}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sum^{L-1}_{k=0}(2^{k}\rho)^{\frac{-sq}{q-1}}\left[\sum^{k}_{j=1}|(u)_{2^{j+1}\rho}-(u)_{2^{j}\rho}|+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}|u(y)-(u)_{2^{k+1}\rho}|^{q-1}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sum^{L-1}_{k=0}(2^{k}\rho)^{\frac{-sq}{q-1}}\sum^{k}_{j=1}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{j+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{j+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{j+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{j+1}\rho}}}|u(y)-(u)_{2^{j+1}\rho}|^{q-1}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sum^{L-1}_{j=0}(2^{j}\rho)^{\frac{-sq}{q-1}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{j+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{j+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{j+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{j+1}\rho}}}|u(y)-(u)_{2^{j+1}\rho}|^{q-1}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}.

(3.15)

Thus, combining (
3
) and (
3
) together with the observations
\tau>s
(thanks to (
2.2
)) and
2^{L}\rho\leq 1
, we obtain

\displaystyle\tilde{I}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum^{L-1}_{k=0}(2^{k}\rho)^{\frac{-q\tau}{q-1}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}|u(y)-(u)_{2^{k+1}\rho}|^{q-1}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}+\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{2^{L}\rho}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{2^{L}\rho}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum^{L-1}_{k=0}(2^{k}\rho)^{\frac{-q\tau}{q-1}}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}|u(y)-g(y)|^{q}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}|g(y)-(g)_{2^{k+1}\rho}|^{q}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{2^{L}\rho}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{2^{L}\rho}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}},

where on the last line we have used the relation (
3.7
). Proceeding similar to (
3.10
) and (
3
), we conclude that

\displaystyle\tilde{I}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum^{L-1}_{k=0}(2^{k}\rho)^{\theta-\frac{q\tau}{q-1}}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p\sigma}}+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{2^{L}\rho}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{2^{L}\rho}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}.

(3.16)

Recalling the definition of
I
from (
3.13
) and using (
3
) with (
3.11
) and (
3
), we have

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\lesssim\rho^{-q\tau+q\theta}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p\sigma}}+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\times\left(\sum^{L-1}_{k=0}(2^{k})^{\theta-\frac{q\tau}{q-1}}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p\sigma}}+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}\right]\right)^{q-1}

\displaystyle\quad+\rho\left[\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p\sigma}}+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\right]\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{2^{L}\rho}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{2^{L}\rho}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right).

(3.17)

Here, by virtue of Young’s inequality, for
\nu\in(0,1)
arbitrary and
\varrho<m-qs
with
m=\min\{p,q\}
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\rho\left[\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p\sigma}}+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\right]\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{2^{L}\rho}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{2^{L}\rho}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)

\displaystyle\leq c(\nu)\rho^{\varrho m}\left[\Big(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dx\Big)^{\frac{1}{\sigma}}+\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx\right]^{\frac{m}{p}}+\nu\left(\rho^{1-\varrho}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{2^{L}\rho}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{2^{L}\rho}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{m^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\nu)\left[1+\Big(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dx\Big)^{\frac{1}{\sigma}}+\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx\right]+\nu\left(\rho^{1-\varrho}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{2^{L}\rho}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{2^{L}\rho}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{p}{m-1}}.

(3.18)

Now combining (
3
) and (
3
), and using Young’s inequality once again (for the first term on the right side of (
3
)), we obtain

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\leq\nu\left[\sum^{L}_{k=1}(2^{k})^{\theta-\frac{q\tau}{q-1}}\left(\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k}\rho}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p\sigma}}+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}\right)\right]^{\frac{p}{\theta}}

\displaystyle\quad+\nu\left(\rho^{1-\varrho}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{2^{L}\rho}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{2^{L}\rho}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{p}{m-1}}

\displaystyle\quad+c(\nu)\left[1+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\sigma}}+\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx\right].

(3.19)

Collecting (
3.10
), (
3.11
), (
3
) and (
3
) in (
3.1
) and using Young’s inequality, we get the required result of (
3.3
) in this case.

Case 2
: If
Q_{\frac{3}{4}\rho}(x_{0})\subset\Omega
holds, then we estimate the right-hand side display of (
3.2
) as below, in order to use Corollary
3.2
. Since
Q_{\frac{29\rho}{40}}\Subset\Omega_{\rho}\subset\Omega
, the first two terms on the right-hand side of (
3.2
) can be estimated directly by applying the Sobolev-Poincaré type inequality of Lemma
2.1
and Corollary
2.2
.
For the nonlocal tail term, noting
Q_{\frac{29\rho}{40}}\subset Q_{\rho}
, an easy computation yields

\displaystyle\tilde{I}_{in}:=\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{\frac{29\rho}{40}}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{\frac{29\rho}{40}}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}\lesssim\rho^{\frac{-qs}{q-1}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{\rho}}}|u-(u)_{\rho}|^{q-1}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}+\tilde{I},

where
\tilde{I}
is as in (
3.13
). Then, following the argument of Case 1, we obtain

\displaystyle\tilde{I}_{in}\leq\sum^{L}_{k=0}(2^{k}\rho)^{\frac{-q\tau}{q-1}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}|u(x)-(u)_{2^{k}\rho}|^{q-1}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}+\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{2^{L}\rho}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{2^{L}\rho}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}.

(3.20)

If
Q_{2^{k}\rho}\subset\Omega
for some
k\leq L
, then by Corollary
2.2
we have

\displaystyle\Bigg(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}|u-(u)_{2^{k}\rho}|^{q-1}\,dx\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}\lesssim\rho^{\theta}\Big(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dx\Big)^{\frac{\theta}{p\sigma}}.

(3.21)

If
Q_{2^{k}\rho}\not\subset\Omega
, then
|Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}\cap\Omega^{c}|>0
and
Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}\Subset\Omega^{\prime}
(thanks to the assumption
Q_{2^{L+1}}\Subset\Omega^{\prime}
). Thus, in this case, by the use of (
3.7
) together with (
3
) and (
3.10
), we obtain

\displaystyle\Bigg(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}|u-(u)_{2^{k}\rho}|^{q-1}\,dx\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Bigg(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}|u-(u)_{2^{k+1}\rho}|^{q}\,dx\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{q}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(2^{k+1}\rho)^{\theta}\Bigg[\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p\sigma}}+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k+1}\rho}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}\Bigg].

(3.22)

Therefore, on account of (
3.21
) and (
3
), we obtain from (
3.20
) that

\displaystyle\tilde{I}_{in}

\displaystyle\leq\sum^{L+1}_{k=1}(2^{k}\rho)^{\theta-\frac{q\tau}{q-1}}\Bigg[\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k}\rho}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p\sigma}}+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}\rho}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{2^{L}\rho}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{2^{L}\rho}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}},

which is a similar estimate to (
3
). Now by following the remaining proof of Case 1 with appropriate modification and recalling (
3.2
), we again have (
3.3
).
∎

Now we prove the main result of this section in the boundary case.

Proposition 3.4

(Higher integrability)
.

Let
\Omega
be an open set satisfying (
2.6
) with
R_{0}>0
and let
u\in\mathbb{X}_{g}(\Omega;\Omega^{\prime})\cap\mathbb{X}(\Omega)
be a weak solution to problem (
1.14
) under the structure assumptions (
1.2
), (
1.3
), (
1.9
) and (
1.10
). Assume that
g\in\mathbb{X}_{{\operatorname{loc}}}(\Omega^{\prime})
and for some small
\varsigma_{0}\in(0,1)
, there holds

\displaystyle|F|\in L^{p(1+\varsigma_{0})}(\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0}))\quad\mbox{and}\quad|Dg|\in L^{p(1+\varsigma_{0})}(Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})),

where
x_{0}\in\overline{\Omega}
and
\rho_{0}\in(0,1)
satisfying
Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})\Subset\Omega^{\prime}
. Then, there exists
\varsigma_{1}\in(0,1)
depending only on
\texttt{data}_{1}
and
\varsigma_{0}
such that for all
\varsigma\in(0,\varsigma_{1})
,
|Du|\in L^{p+\varsigma}(\Omega_{\rho_{0}}(x_{0}))
. Moreover, there exists a constant
c=c(\texttt{data}_{1},\varsigma_{0})
such that

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}|Du|^{p+\varsigma}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p+\varsigma}}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}|Du|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+c\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}|Dg|^{p(1+\varsigma)}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p(1+\varsigma)}}

\displaystyle\quad+c\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}(|F|^{p}+1)^{1+\varsigma}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p(1+\varsigma)}}+c\left(\rho_{0}^{1-\varrho}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{3\rho_{0}}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{m-1}}

for all
\varrho\in(0,m-qs)
with
m=\min\{p,q\}
, where we have set

\displaystyle\texttt{data}_{1}:=\texttt{data}_{1}\big(\texttt{data},\mathcal{M}(u;\Omega),\mathcal{M}(g;Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0}))\big).

Proof.

Proceeding with the notations of the proposition, for
Q_{R}(x)\subset Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})
, we define the following functionals:

\displaystyle\Upsilon(x,R):=\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}(x)}}(|F|^{p}+1)^{1+\varsigma}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p(1+\varsigma)}}+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{R}(x)}}|Dg|^{p(1+\varsigma)}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p(1+\varsigma)}},

\displaystyle\Psi_{M}(x,R):=\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}(x)}}|Du|^{p}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+M\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}(x)}}(|F|^{p}+1)\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+M\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{R}(x)}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p}},

where
\varsigma\in(0,1)
and
M\geq 1
are constants which will be determined later, and

\displaystyle{\rm Tail}(x,R):

\displaystyle=\left[\sum_{k=1}^{l+1}\beta_{k}\Bigg(\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2^{k}R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k}R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k}R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k}R}(x)}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dy\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p\sigma}}+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k}R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}R}(x)}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}\Bigg)\right]^{\frac{1}{\theta}}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(R^{1-\varrho}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{2^{l}R}(x)}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{2^{l}R}|^{q-1}}{|y-x|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{m-1}},

(3.23)

where
\beta_{k}:=(2^{k})^{\theta-\frac{q\tau}{q-1}}
,
\varrho<q(1-s)
and
l=l(R)\in\mathbb{N}
is such that
\frac{\rho_{0}}{2}\leq 2^{l}R<\rho_{0}
. Moreover, we set

\displaystyle\Xi(x,R):=\Upsilon(x,R)+\Psi_{M}(x,R)+{\rm Tail}(x,R)\quad\mbox{and}

\displaystyle\Xi_{0}(x,R):=\Upsilon(x,R)+\Psi_{1}(x,R)+\left(R^{1-\varrho}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{R}(x)}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{R}|^{q-1}}{|y-x|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{m-1}}.

Fix
r_{1}
and
r_{2}
such that
\frac{\rho_{0}}{2}\leq r_{1}<r_{2}\leq\rho_{0}
, and define

\displaystyle\lambda_{0}:=\sup\left\{\Xi(x,R)\ :\ x\in\Omega_{r_{1}}(x_{0}),\ \frac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{16}<R\leq\frac{\rho_{0}}{2}\right\}.

(3.24)

Then the proof is divided into five steps as below.

Step 1
:
An upper bound on
\lambda_{0}
. For any
x\in\Omega_{r_{1}}(x_{0})
and
\frac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{16}<R\leq\frac{\rho_{0}}{2}
, we observe that

\displaystyle\Upsilon(x,R)\leq\left(\frac{3\rho_{0}}{R}\right)^{\frac{n}{p(1+\varsigma)}}\Upsilon(x_{0},3\rho_{0})\leq\left(16\frac{3\rho_{0}}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{n}{p}}\Upsilon(x_{0},3\rho_{0}).

Additionally, for
k=0,\dots,l+1
, noting
Q_{2^{l+1}R}(x)\subset Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})
, we have

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2^{k}R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k}R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k}R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2^{k}R}(x)}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p\sigma}}\leq\left(16\frac{3\rho_{0}}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{n+p}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}|Du|^{p}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

for all
\frac{n+p}{n}\leq\sigma\leq 1
and

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2^{k}R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}R}(x)}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2^{k}R}(x)}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\leq\left(16\frac{3\rho_{0}}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{n}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}.

Furthermore, for the nonlocal tail term, similar to the proof of Lemma
3.3
, we see that

\displaystyle J_{0}:

\displaystyle=\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{2^{l}R}(x)}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{2^{l}R}|^{q-1}}{|y-x|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{m-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{3\rho_{0}}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{n+sq}{m-1}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{3\rho_{0}}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{m-1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{3\rho_{0}}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{2n+sq}{m-1}}(3\rho_{0})^{-\frac{sq}{m-1}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}|u(y)-(u)_{3\rho_{0}}|^{q-1}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{m-1}}.

Noting that
m\leq p
and
u-g\in L^{\infty}(Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0}))
if
q>m
, by Hölder’s inequality and Poincaré’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}|u(y)-(u)_{3\rho_{0}}|^{q-1}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{m-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u-g\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{3\rho_{0}})}^{\frac{q-m}{m-1}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}|u(y)-(u)_{3\rho_{0}}|^{m-1}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{m-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\rho_{0}\left(\Big(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}|Du|^{p}\,dy\Big)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\Big(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy\Big)^{\frac{1}{p}}\right),

where we have also used (
3.7
).
Combining the above estimates and noting that
\varrho<m-qs
, we obtain

\displaystyle\lambda_{0}\leq cM\left(\frac{3\rho_{0}}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{2n+sq}{m-1}+\frac{n+p}{p}}\,\Xi_{0}(x_{0},3\rho_{0}).

(3.25)

Step 2
:
Vitali covering
. For
\lambda\geq\lambda_{0}
and
t>0
, we define

\displaystyle E_{\lambda}^{t}:=\Big\{x\in\Omega_{t}(x_{0})\,:\,\sup_{0\leq R\leq\frac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{16}}\Psi_{M}(x,R)>\lambda\Big\}.

(3.26)

From (
3.24
), it is clear that for each
x\in\Omega_{r_{1}}(x_{0})
and
R\in\big[\frac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{16},\frac{\rho_{0}}{2}\big]
, there holds

\displaystyle\Psi_{M}(x,R)\leq\lambda_{0}\leq\lambda.

Moreover, for each
x\in E_{\lambda}^{r_{1}}
with
\lambda\geq\lambda_{0}
, there exists
R_{x}\in(0,\frac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{16}\big]
such that

\displaystyle\Psi_{M}(x,R_{x})\geq\lambda\quad\mbox{and}\quad\Psi_{M}(x,R)\leq\lambda\quad\mbox{for all }R\in(R_{x},\tfrac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{16}\big].

(3.27)

By Vitali’s covering lemma, there exists a sequence of pairwise disjoint cylinders
\{Q_{2R_{x_{j}}}(x_{j})\}
such that

\displaystyle E_{\lambda}^{r_{1}}\subset\bigcup_{j=1}^{\infty}\Omega_{10R_{x_{j}}}(x_{j}).

(3.28)

For each
j\in\mathbb{N}
, setting
R_{j}:=R_{x_{j}}
and
Q_{j}:=Q_{R_{x_{j}}}(x_{j})
, we choose
l_{j}\in\mathbb{N}
satisfying

\displaystyle\frac{\rho_{0}}{2}\leq 2^{l_{j}}R_{j}<\rho_{0}.

(3.29)

Since
R_{j}\leq\frac{1}{16}\frac{\rho_{0}}{2}
, it is evident that
l_{j}\geq 3
for all
j\in\mathbb{N}
. Moreover,
Q_{2^{l_{j}+1}R_{j}}(x_{j})\subset Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0})
.

Step 3
:
Estimate on
|\Omega_{j}|
. On account of (
3.26
), either of the following holds:

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{j}}}|Du|^{p}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\geq\frac{\lambda}{2}\quad\mbox{or}

(3.30)

\displaystyle M\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{j}}}(|F|^{p}+1)\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+M\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{j}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\geq\frac{\lambda}{2}.

(3.31)

Case 1
: Suppose that (
3.30
) holds. Then,
by the reverse Hölder inequality (
3.3
), we have

\displaystyle\lambda^{p}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt2\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2\Omega_{j}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{\sigma}}+c\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt2\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2\Omega_{j}}}(|F|^{p}+1)\,dy+c\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt2Q_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2Q_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2Q_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2Q_{j}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy

\displaystyle\quad+\nu\left[\sum^{l_{j}+1}_{k=1}\beta_{k}\left(\Big(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt2^{k}\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{k}\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{k}\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{k}\Omega_{j}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dy\Big)^{\frac{\theta}{p\sigma}}+\Big(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt2^{k}Q_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{k}Q_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{k}Q_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{k}Q_{j}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy\Big)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}\right)\right]^{\frac{p}{\theta}}

\displaystyle\quad+\nu\left(R_{j}^{1-\varrho}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus 2^{l_{j}}Q_{j}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{2^{l_{j}}R_{j}}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{p}{m-1}},

where
aQ_{j}=Q_{aR_{x_{j}}}(x_{j})
for
a>0
. As a consequence of (
3.27
), for all
k=1,\dots,l_{j}-1
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt2^{k}\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{k}\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{k}\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{k}\Omega_{j}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p\sigma}}\leq\lambda,\quad\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt2^{k}Q_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{k}Q_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{k}Q_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{k}Q_{j}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\leq\frac{\lambda}{M}

and

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt2\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2\Omega_{j}}}(|F|^{p}+1)\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\leq\frac{\lambda}{M}.

Next, recalling the definition of
\mathrm{Tail}(x_{j},R)
from (
3
) for
R=2^{l_{j}-1}R_{j}\in\big[\frac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{16},\frac{\rho_{0}}{2}\big]
(with
l_{R}=1
), on account of (
3.24
), we have

\displaystyle\beta_{l_{j}}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}

\displaystyle\left[\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt2^{l_{j}}\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{l_{j}}\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{l_{j}}\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{l_{j}}\Omega_{j}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dy\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p\sigma}}+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt2^{l_{j}}Q_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{l_{j}}Q_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{l_{j}}Q_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{l_{j}}Q_{R}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\beta_{l_{j}}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt2^{l_{j}+1}\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{l_{j}+1}\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{l_{j}+1}\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{l_{j}+1}\Omega_{j}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dy\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p\sigma}}+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt2^{l_{j}+1}Q_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{l_{j}+1}Q_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{l_{j}+1}Q_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2^{l_{j}+1}Q_{R}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}\right]^{\frac{1}{\theta}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\left[(2^{l_{j}}R_{j})^{1-\varrho}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus 2^{l_{j}}Q_{j}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{2^{l_{j}}R_{j}}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right]^{\frac{1}{m-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{x\in\Omega_{r_{1}}(x_{0})}{\rm Tail}(x,2^{l_{j}-1}R_{j})\lesssim\lambda.

Consequently, for
c=c(\texttt{data}_{1})
, since
\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\beta_{k}=c<\infty
(thanks to (
2.2
)), we obtain

\displaystyle\lambda^{p}\leq c(\nu)\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt2\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{2\Omega_{j}}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{\sigma}}+c\frac{\lambda^{p}}{M^{p}}+c\nu\lambda^{p}.

Now, for suitable choice of
\nu
(small enough) and
M
(large enough), both depending only on
\texttt{data}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle|\Omega_{j}|\leq\frac{c}{\lambda^{p\sigma}}\int_{2\Omega_{j}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dy,

which after simple manipulation implies that

\displaystyle|\Omega_{j}|\leq\frac{c}{\lambda^{p\sigma}}\int_{2\Omega_{j}\cap\{|Du|>\kappa\lambda\}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dy,

(3.32)

for some
\kappa\in(0,1)
small enough which is independent of
\lambda
and
j
.

Case 2
: Now suppose that (
3.31
) holds, then either

\displaystyle M\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{j}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\geq\frac{\lambda}{4}\quad\mbox{or}\quad M\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{j}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{j}}}(|F|^{p}+1)\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\geq\frac{\lambda}{4}.

(3.33)

We now assume that the first inequality of (
3.33
) holds true. As a consequence, with some elementary manipulations, we have

\displaystyle|Q_{j}|\leq c\left(\frac{M}{\lambda}\right)^{p}\int_{2Q_{j}\cap\{|Dg|>\hat{\kappa}\lambda\}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy,

(3.34)

for some
\hat{\kappa}>0
small enough that depends only on
\texttt{data}_{1}
. If the second inequality of (
3.33
) holds, then we can proceed similarly to obtain

\displaystyle|\Omega_{j}|\leq c\left(\frac{M}{\lambda}\right)^{p}\int_{2\Omega_{j}\cap\{(|F|^{p}+1)^{1/p}>\tilde{\kappa}\lambda\}}(|F|^{p}+1)\,dy

(3.35)

for some small
\tilde{\kappa}>0
that depends only on
\texttt{data}_{1}
. Combining (
3.32
), (
3.34
) and (
3.35
), we get

\displaystyle|\Omega_{j}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c}{\lambda^{p\sigma}}\int_{2\Omega_{j}\cap\{|Du|>\kappa\lambda\}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dy+\frac{c}{\lambda^{p}}\int_{2Q_{j}\cap\{|Dg|>\hat{\kappa}\lambda\}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{c}{\lambda^{p}}\int_{2\Omega_{j}\cap\{(|F|^{p}+1)^{1/p}>\tilde{\kappa}\lambda\}}(|F|^{p}+1)\,dy.

(3.36)

Step 4
:
The good
\lambda
inequality
.
Since
\{2Q_{j}\}
is a collection of pairwise disjoint sets and
\cup_{j=1}^{\infty}2Q_{j}\subset Q_{r_{2}}(x_{0})
, (
3
) yields

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}|\Omega_{j}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c}{\lambda^{p\sigma}}\int_{\Omega_{r_{2}}(x_{0})\cap\{|Du|>\kappa\lambda\}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dy+\frac{c}{\lambda^{p}}\int_{Q_{r_{2}}(x_{0})\cap\{|Dg|>\hat{\kappa}\lambda\}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{c}{\lambda^{p}}\int_{\Omega_{r_{2}}(x_{0})\cap\{(|F|^{p}+1)^{1/p}>\tilde{\kappa}\lambda\}}(|F|^{p}+1)\,dy.

(3.37)

After some elementary manipulations and using (
3.29
) along with (
3
) and (
3.28
) (up to relabeling the constants
\hat{\kappa}
and
\tilde{\kappa}
), we deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega_{r_{1}}(x_{0})\cap\{|Du|>\lambda\}}|Du|^{p}\,dy

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c}{\lambda^{p\sigma-p}}\int_{\Omega_{r_{2}}(x_{0})\cap\{|Du|>\lambda\}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dy+c\int_{Q_{r_{2}}(x_{0})\cap\{|Dg|>\hat{\kappa}\lambda\}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy

\displaystyle\quad+c\int_{\Omega_{r_{2}}(x_{0})\cap\{(|F|^{p}+1)^{1/p}>\tilde{\kappa}\lambda\}}(|F|^{p}+1)\,dy.

(3.38)

Step 5
:
Conclusion
.
Now the rest of the proof follows by standard arguments using (
3
). Indeed, for
t\geq 0
, we define the truncated function

\displaystyle[|Du|]_{t}:=\min\{|Du|,t\}.

Then, for
\varsigma\in(0,1)
, to be determined later, we have (e.g., see
[
48
]
)

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega_{r_{1}}(x_{0})}[|Du|]_{t}^{\varsigma}\,|Du|^{p}\,dy\leq\lambda_{0}^{\varsigma}\int_{\Omega_{r_{1}}(x_{0})}|Du|^{p}\,dy+\varsigma\int_{\lambda_{0}}^{t}\lambda^{\varsigma-1}\int_{\Omega_{r_{1}}(x_{0})\cap\{|Du|\geq\lambda\}}|Du|^{p}\,dy\,d\lambda.

The above expression with the help of (
3
) and the definition of
\lambda_{0}
imply that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega_{r_{1}}(x_{0})}

\displaystyle[|Du|]_{t}^{\varsigma}\,|Du|^{p}\,dy

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda_{0}^{\varsigma+p}|\Omega_{r_{1}}(x_{0})|+c\,\varsigma\int_{\lambda_{0}}^{t}\lambda^{\varsigma-1+p-p\sigma}\int_{\Omega_{r_{2}}(x_{0})\cap\{|Du|>\lambda\}}|Du|^{p\sigma}\,dy\,d\lambda

\displaystyle\quad+c\,\varsigma\int_{\lambda_{0}}^{t}\lambda^{\varsigma-1}\int_{Q_{r_{2}}(x_{0})\cap\{|Dg|>\hat{\kappa}\lambda\}}|Dg|^{p}\,dy\,d\lambda

\displaystyle\quad+c\,\varsigma\int_{\lambda_{0}}^{t}\lambda^{\varsigma-1}\int_{\Omega_{r_{2}}(x_{0})\cap\{(|F|^{p}+1)^{1/p}>\tilde{\kappa}\lambda\}}(|F|^{p}+1)\,dy\,d\lambda.

Now, by using the upper bound on
\lambda_{0}
given by (
3.25
) and Fubini’s theorem (see
[
48
]
for details), we can deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega_{r_{1}}(x_{0})}

\displaystyle[|Du|]_{t}^{\varsigma}\,|Du|^{p}\,dy

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c\,\varsigma}{p-p\sigma+\varsigma}\int_{\Omega_{r_{2}}(x_{0})}[|Du|]_{t}^{\varsigma}\,|Du|^{p}\,dy+c|Q_{r_{2}}(x_{0})|\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{r_{2}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{r_{2}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{r_{2}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{r_{2}}(x_{0})}}|Dg|^{p+\varsigma}\,dy

\displaystyle\quad+c|\Omega_{r_{2}}(x_{0})|\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{r_{2}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{r_{2}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{r_{2}}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{r_{2}}(x_{0})}}(|F|^{p}+1)^{1+\varsigma}\,dy+c\ \Xi_{0}(x_{0},3\rho_{0})^{p+\varsigma}|\Omega_{r_{1}}(x_{0})|.

Choosing
\varsigma_{1}\in(0,\varsigma_{0})
small enough depending only on
\texttt{data}_{1}
,
\varsigma_{0}
and
\sigma
(as the constant
c
depends only on
\texttt{data}_{1}
) such that
\frac{c\,\varsigma_{1}}{p-p\sigma}\leq\frac{1}{16}
, from a standard iteration argument, for all
\varsigma<\varsigma_{1}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\Omega_{\rho_{0}/2}(x_{0})}[|Du|]_{t}^{\varsigma}\,|Du|^{p}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p+\varsigma}}\leq c\ \Xi_{0}(x_{0},3\rho_{0}),

which upon taking the limit as
t\to\infty
yields

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\Omega_{\rho_{0}/2}(x_{0})}|Du|^{p+\varsigma}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p+\varsigma}}\leq c\ \Xi_{0}(x_{0},3\rho_{0}).

(3.39)

To complete the proof of the proposition, we use a covering argument as follows. For
r=\frac{4\rho_{0}}{5}
, there exist finitely many points
x_{1},\dots,x_{k}\in Q_{\frac{\rho_{0}}{2}}(x_{0})
such that

\displaystyle\overline{Q_{\frac{9}{10}\rho_{0}}(x_{0})}\subset\bigcup_{i=1}^{k}Q_{\frac{r}{2}}(x_{i})\quad\mbox{and}\quad Q_{3r}(x_{i})\Subset Q_{3\rho_{0}}(x_{0}).

(3.40)

Therefore, using (
3.39
) for each
(x_{i},r)
, we get

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\Omega_{r/2}(x_{i})}|Du|^{p+\varsigma}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p+\varsigma}}\leq c\ \Xi_{0}(x_{i},3r)\leq c(k)\ \Xi(x_{0},3\rho_{0}),

where the last inequality follows from the assertion of Step 1. Noting (
3.40
) and summing over
i=1
to
k
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\Omega_{9\rho_{0}/10}(x_{0})}|Du|^{p+\varsigma}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{p+\varsigma}}\leq c(k)\ \Xi(x_{0},3\rho_{0}).

Now, the conclusion of the proposition follows by repeating the above procedure for a different choice of
r
, for instance,
r=\rho_{0}/10
.
∎

4.
Interior Gradient Estimate

Throughout the section, we assume that
u
is a local weak solution to (
1.7
) with
|F|\in L^{p}(\Omega)
. For
fixed
\tilde{\Omega}\Subset\Omega
, we select
R\in(0,1/9)
and choose
L=L(R)\in\mathbb{N}
such that
3^{L}R\leq 1
and
Q_{3^{L}R}\Subset\tilde{\Omega}
.

Consider the problem:

\left\{\begin{array}[]{rlll}-\textnormal{div}(A(x,Dw))&=0\quad\text{in}\,\,Q_{2R},\\
w&=u\quad\text{on}\,\,\partial Q_{2R}.\end{array}\right.

(4.1)

Before giving the first comparison estimate, we mention the following energy estimate and maximum principle which are local analogues of (
5.1
) and (
5.5
), respectively.

Lemma 4.1
.

Let
w
be a weak solution to problem (
4.1
), then there exists
c=c(n,p,\Lambda)
such that

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}|Dw|^{p}\,dx\leq c\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx,

and for the case
p<q<\frac{p}{s}
, there holds

\displaystyle\|w\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2R})}\leq c\|u\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{2R})}.

Now we have the following comparison lemma.

Lemma 4.2
.

For every
\varepsilon
,
\nu\in(0,1)
and
\varrho<m-qs
, there holds

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}|V(Du)-V(Dw)|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle\leq(\varepsilon+c(\nu)R^{\Bbbk})\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx+c(\varepsilon,\nu)\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}(|F|^{p}+1)\,dx+\nu\left[\sum^{L}_{k=2}\beta_{k}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3^{k}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k}R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}\right]^{\frac{p}{\theta}}

\displaystyle\quad+\nu\left(R^{1-\varrho}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{3^{L}R}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{3^{L}R}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{p}{m-1}},

where
m:=\min\{p,q\}
,
\theta
is as in (
2.1
),
\tau
is given by (
2.2
),
\Bbbk:=\min\{q(\theta-\tau),\varrho p\}>0
,
\beta_{k}:=3^{k({\theta-\frac{q\tau}{q-1}})}
with
\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\beta_{k}<\infty
, and
c(\varepsilon,\nu)=c(\texttt{data},\mathcal{M}(u;\tilde{\Omega}),\varepsilon,\nu)
(analogously
c(\nu)
is defined).

Remark 4.3
.

Note that the term

\displaystyle\sum^{L}_{k=2}\beta_{k}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3^{k}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k}R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}

is taken to be zero if
L=1
.

Proof.

We first observe that
u-w\in W^{1,p}_{0}(Q_{2R})
. Thus, it can be extended to
\tilde{\Omega}
by setting
u-w=0
outside
Q_{2R}
. Moreover, for the case
q>p
,
u\in L^{\infty}(\tilde{\Omega})
and hence
u-w\in L^{\infty}(\tilde{\Omega})
by Lemma
4.1
. Consequently, by Lemma
2.3
,
u-w\in W^{1,p}(Q_{3R})\cap W^{s,q}(Q_{3R})
is an admissible test function for (
1.7
) and (
4.1
). Therefore, with the help of (
2.7
), we obtain

\displaystyle\Lambda^{-1}\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}|V(Du)-V(Dw)|^{2}\,dx\lesssim\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}(A(x,Du)-A(x,Dw))\cdot(Du-Dw)\,dx

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim|Q_{2R}|^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}|u(x)-u(y)|^{q-1}|u(x)-w(x)-u(y)+w(y)|K(x,y)\,dx\,dy

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}|F|^{p-1}|D(u-w)|\,dx=:I+J.

By Young’s inequality, for
\varepsilon>0
we get

\displaystyle J\leq\varepsilon\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}|Du-Dw|^{p}\,dx+c(\varepsilon)\int_{Q_{2R}}|F|^{p}\,dx.

(4.2)

To estimate
I
, we split the integral into two parts as below:

\displaystyle I_{1}:=\left(\dfrac{3}{2}\right)^{n}\Lambda\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}\int_{Q_{3R}}\dfrac{|u(x)-u(y)|^{q-1}}{|x-y|^{n+sq}}|u(x)-w(x)-u(y)+w(y)|\,dx\,dy

and

\displaystyle I_{2}:=2\Lambda\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{3R}}\dfrac{|u(x)-u(y)|^{q-1}}{|x-y|^{n+sq}}|u(x)-w(x)|\,dx\,dy.

For
I_{1}
, applying Hölder’s inequality, Lemma
2.3
, Lemma
4.1
and Young’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}\int_{Q_{3R}}\dfrac{|u(x)-u(y)|^{q}}{|x-y|^{n+sq}}\,dx\,dy\right)^{\frac{q-1}{q}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}\int_{Q_{3R}}\dfrac{|u(x)-w(x)-u(y)+w(y)|^{q}}{|x-y|^{n+sq}}\,dx\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}

\displaystyle\lesssim R^{(q-1)(\theta-s)}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta(q-1)}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|Du-Dw|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}R^{(\theta-s)}\|u\|^{(1-\theta)q}_{L^{\infty}(Q_{3R})}

\displaystyle\lesssim R^{q(\theta-s)}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta q}{p}}\lesssim R^{q(\theta-s)}\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}(|Du|^{p}+1)\,dx.

(4.3)

Next, similar to (
3.5
), for
x\in Q_{2R}\equiv Q_{2R}(x_{0})
and
y\in Q^{c}_{3R}\equiv Q^{c}_{3R}(x_{0})
, we have

\displaystyle|x-y|\geq\dfrac{3-2\sqrt{2}}{3}|y-x_{0}|.

Consequently,

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{3R}}\dfrac{|u(x)-(u)_{3R}|^{q-1}+|u(y)-(u)_{3R}|^{q-1}}{|x_{0}-y|^{n+sq}}|u(x)-w(x)|\,dx\,dy

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|u(x)-(u)_{3R}|^{q-1}|u(x)-w(x)|\,dx\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{3R}}\dfrac{1}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy

\displaystyle\quad+\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|u(x)-w(x)|\,dx\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{3R}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{3R}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy

\displaystyle=:I_{3}+I_{4}.

(4.4)

Using Hölder’s inequality, Corollary
2.2
and Lemma
4.1
, we get

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim R^{-sq}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|u(x)-(u)_{3R}|^{q}\,dx\right)^{\frac{q-1}{q}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{2R}}}|u(x)-w(x)|^{q}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}
