\displaystyle\lesssim R^{q(\theta-s)}\left[\|u\|^{1-\theta}_{L^{\infty}(Q_{3R})}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}\right]^{q-1}\|u-w\|^{1-\theta}_{L^{\infty}(Q_{3R})}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|Du-Dw|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim R^{q(\theta-s)}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta q}{p}}\lesssim R^{q(\theta-s)}\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}(|Du|^{p}+1)\,dx,

(4.5)

where for the last relation we have used Young’s inequality.

Next, set

\displaystyle\tilde{I}_{4}:=\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{3R}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{3R}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}.

Then, proceeding as in (
3
) and (
3
), and using Corollary
2.2
we obtain

\displaystyle\tilde{I}_{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum^{L-1}_{k=1}(3^{k}R)^{\frac{-q\tau}{q-1}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3^{k+1}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k+1}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k+1}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k+1}R}}}|u(y)-(u)_{3^{k+1}R}|^{q}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}+\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{3^{L}R}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{3^{L}R}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum^{L-1}_{k=1}(3^{k}R)^{\theta-\frac{q\tau}{q-1}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}(\tilde{\Omega})}^{(1-\theta)}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3^{k+1}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k+1}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k+1}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k+1}R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}+\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{3^{L}R}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{3^{L}R}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q-1}},

(4.6)

where we have additionally used the facts that
Q_{3^{L}R}\Subset\tilde{\Omega}
,
3^{L}R\leq 1
and
\tau>s
.
Moreover, as in
I_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|u(x)-w(x)|\,dx\leq cR^{\theta}\|u\|^{1-\theta}_{L^{\infty}(Q_{3R})}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}.

(4.7)

Coupling (
4
) and (
4.7
), and applying Lemma
2.6
with Lemma
4.1
, we have

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim R^{-q\tau+q\theta}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}\left(\sum^{L-1}_{k=1}(3^{k})^{\theta-\frac{q\tau}{q-1}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3^{k+1}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k+1}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k+1}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k+1}R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}\right)^{q-1}

\displaystyle\quad+R\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{3^{L}R}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{3^{L}R}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right).

Then, similar to (
3
), using Young’s inequality and recalling the definition of various constants defined in the Lemma, we obtain

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\nu\left[\sum^{L-1}_{k=1}(3^{k})^{\theta-\frac{q\tau}{q-1}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3^{k+1}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k+1}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k+1}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k+1}R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}\right]^{\frac{p}{\theta}}

\displaystyle\quad+c(\nu)+c(\nu)R^{\Bbbk}\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx+\nu\left(R^{1-\varrho}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{3^{L}R}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{3^{L}R}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{p}{m-1}}.

(4.8)

Finally, by using (
4
) and (
4
) in (
4
), and combining it with (
4
) and (
4.2
), we conclude the proof of the lemma.
∎

Proof of Theorem
1.3
: The proof is similar to the boundary case Theorem
1.6
, which will be presented in detail in the next section. Indeed, we use the comparison estimate of Lemma
4.2
instead of Lemma
5.1
, Lemma
5.2
for the case
Q_{R}\Subset\Omega
and the similar covering argument as in Subsection
5.2
without the

-term.∎

5.
Global Gradient Estimate

Throughout this section we assume that
u\in\mathbb{X}_{g}(\Omega;\Omega^{\prime})\cap\mathbb{X}(\Omega)
is the weak solution of (
1.14
) with
|F|\in L^{\gamma}(\Omega)
,
g\in\mathbb{X}(\Omega^{\prime})
and
|Dg|\in L^{\gamma}(\Omega^{\prime})
. Also,
\Omega
is
(\delta,r_{*})
-Reifenberg flat where
\delta\in(0,\frac{1}{2^{n+1}})
and
r_{*}>0
are determined later. With the notation of (
1.6
), we set

\displaystyle\texttt{data}_{2}:=\texttt{data}_{2}\big(\texttt{data},\mathcal{M}(u;\Omega),\mathcal{M}(g;\Omega^{\prime})\big).

5.1
.
The Comparison Estimates

Let
x_{0}\in\overline{\Omega}
and
R\in(0,1/9)
such that
Q_{2R}(x_{0})\not\subset\Omega
and
Q_{3R}(x_{0})\Subset\Omega^{\prime}
. For the convenience, we abbreviate

\Omega_{R}=\Omega_{R}(x_{0}).

Let
h_{b}\in W^{1,p}(\Omega_{2R})
be the weak solution of

\left\{\begin{array}[]{rlll}-\textnormal{div}(A(x,Dh_{b}))&=0&&\text{in}\quad\Omega_{2R},\\
h_{b}&=u-g&&\text{on}\quad\partial(\Omega_{2R}).\end{array}\right.

(5.1)

Then, we have the following standard energy estimate

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}|Dh_{b}|^{p}\,dx\leq c\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx+c\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx

(5.2)

with
c=c(n,p,\Lambda)
. Indeed, by testing
h_{b}-u+g\in W^{1,p}_{0}(\Omega_{2R})
to (
5.1
),

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}A(x,Dh_{b})\cdot D(h_{b}-u+g)\,dx=0

holds and so by (
1.9
) and Young’s inequality, we find

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}|Dh_{b}|^{p}\,dx

\displaystyle\leq c\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}|Dh_{b}|^{p-1}|Du|\,dx+c\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}|Dh_{b}|^{p-1}|Dg|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq c\,\varepsilon\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}|Dh_{b}|^{p}\,dx+c(\varepsilon)\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx+c(\varepsilon)\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx

for any
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
with
c=c(n,p,\Lambda)
and
c(\varepsilon)=c(n,p,\Lambda,\varepsilon)>0
. In the above display, by choosing
\varepsilon=\varepsilon(n,p,\Lambda)
sufficiently small and absorbing the first term of the right-hand side to the left-hand side, we obtain (
5.2
).

We also have the following self-improving property for
Dh_{b}
(see
[
43
]
):

•

Let
\varsigma_{1}\in(0,1)
be given by Proposition
3.4
. Then, there exists
\sigma_{0}=\sigma_{0}(n,p,\Lambda,\varsigma_{1})>0
, small enough such that for all

, there holds

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}|Dh_{b}|^{p(1+\sigma)}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p(1+\sigma)}}&\leq c\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}|Dh_{b}|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+c\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}|Du|^{p(1+\sigma)}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p(1+\sigma)}}\\
&\quad+c\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}|Dg|^{p(1+\sigma)}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p(1+\sigma)}}\end{split}

(5.3)

with
c=c(n,p,\Lambda)>0
.

We now prove the first comparison lemma.

Lemma 5.1
.

Let
L\in\mathbb{N}
be such that
3^{L}R\leq 1
and
Q_{3^{L}R}\Subset\Omega^{\prime}
.
Then for every
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
and
\nu\in(0,1)
, there holds

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}|V(Du)-V(Dh_{b})|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad\leq(\varepsilon+c(\nu)R^{\Bbbk})\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx+c(\varepsilon,\nu)\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3R}}}(|F|^{p}+1)\,dx

\displaystyle\quad\quad+c(\varepsilon,\nu)\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx+\nu\left(R^{1-\varrho}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{3^{L}R}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{3^{L}R}|^{q-1}}{|y-x_{0}|^{n+sq}}\,dy\right)^{\frac{p}{m-1}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\nu\left[\sum^{L}_{k=2}\beta_{k}\left(\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3^{k}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3^{k}R}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{3^{k}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3^{k}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3^{k}R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3^{k}R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}\right)\right]^{\frac{p}{\theta}},

(5.4)

where
\Bbbk,\varrho,m,\beta_{k}
and
\theta
are as in Lemma
4.2
, and
c(\varepsilon,\nu)=c(\texttt{data}_{2},\varepsilon,\nu)
(analogously
c(\nu)
is defined).

Proof.

Set
w:=u-g-h_{b}\in W^{1,p}_{0}(\Omega_{2R})
. We extend
w
by
0
outside
\Omega_{2R}
. Moreover, for the case
q>p
, since we have assumed that
u\in L^{\infty}(\Omega)
and
g\in L^{\infty}(\Omega_{2R})
, by the standard maximum principle,

\displaystyle\|h_{b}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega_{2R})}\lesssim\|u\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega_{2R})}+\|g\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega_{2R})}

(5.5)

holds with
c=c(n,p,\Lambda)
and so
w\in L^{\infty}(\Omega_{2R})
. Thus, by Lemma
2.3
, it can easily be verified that
w\in\mathbb{X}_{0}(\Omega;\Omega^{\prime})
.
Taking
w
as a test function in the weak formulation of (
1.14
) and (
5.1
), we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega_{2R}}\left(A(x,Du)-A(x,Dh_{b})\right)\cdot Dw\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}|u(x)-u(y)|^{q-2}(u(x)-u(y))(w(x)-w(y))K(x,y)\,dx\,dy

\displaystyle\quad\quad=\int_{\Omega_{2R}}|F|^{p-2}F\cdot Dw\,dx.

Then (
1.9
) and (
1.10
) yield

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega_{2R}}|V(Du)-V(Dh_{b})|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle\eqsim\int_{\Omega_{2R}}\left(A(x,Du)-A(x,Dh_{b})\right)\cdot D(u-h_{b})\,dx

\displaystyle=\int_{\Omega_{2R}}|F|^{p-2}F\cdot Dw\,dx+\int_{\Omega_{2R}}\big(A(x,Du)-A(x,Dh_{b})\big)\cdot Dg\,dx

\displaystyle\ +\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}|u(x)-u(y)|^{q-2}(u(x)-u(y))(w(x)-w(y))K(x,y)\,dx\,dy

\displaystyle\lesssim\underbrace{\int_{\Omega_{2R}}|F|^{p-1}|Dw|\,dx+\int_{\Omega_{2R}}(|A(x,Du)+|A(x,Dh_{b})|)|Dg|\,dx}_{=:I}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\dfrac{|u(x)-u(y)|^{q-1}|w(x)-w(y)|}{|x-y|^{n+sq}}\,dx\,dy}_{=:J}.

Using Young’s inequality and (
5.2
), we have

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\leq c(\varepsilon)\int_{\Omega_{2R}}|F|^{p}\,dx+\varepsilon\int_{\Omega_{2R}}|Dw|^{p}\,dx+c(\varepsilon)\int_{\Omega_{2R}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx+\varepsilon\int_{\Omega_{2R}}(|Du|^{p}+|Dh_{b}|^{p})\,dx

\displaystyle\lesssim c(\varepsilon)\int_{\Omega_{2R}}|F|^{p}\,dx+c(\varepsilon)\int_{\Omega_{2R}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx+\varepsilon\int_{\Omega_{2R}}|Du|^{p}\,dx

for any
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
with
c(\varepsilon)=c(n,p,\Lambda,\varepsilon)>0
. For the nonlocal term, we write

\displaystyle J

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\int_{Q_{3R}}\int_{Q_{3R}}\dfrac{|u(x)-u(y)|^{q-1}|w(x)-w(y)|}{|x-y|^{n+sq}}\,dxdy}_{=:J_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{2\int_{Q_{2R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{3R}}\dfrac{|u(x)-u(y)|^{q-1}}{|x-y|^{n+sq}}|w(x)|\,dydx}_{=:J_{2}}.

Then, by Hölder’s inequality, Lemma
2.3
, (
5.2
) and (
5.5
), we estimate

\displaystyle J_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{Q_{3R}}\int_{Q_{3R}}\dfrac{|u(x)-g(x)-u(y)+g(y)|^{q-1}}{|x-y|^{n+sq}}|w(x)-w(y)|\,dx\,dy

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{Q_{3R}}\int_{Q_{3R}}\dfrac{|g(x)-g(y)|^{q-1}}{|x-y|^{n+sq}}|w(x)-w(y)|\,dx\,dy

\displaystyle\lesssim|Q_{3R}|\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}\int_{Q_{3R}}\dfrac{|u(x)-g(x)-u(y)+g(y)|^{q}}{|x-y|^{n+sq}}\,dxdy\right)^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}\int_{Q_{3R}}\dfrac{|w(x)-w(y)|^{q}}{|x-y|^{n+sq}}\,dxdy\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}

\displaystyle\quad+|Q_{3R}|\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}\int_{Q_{3R}}\dfrac{|g(x)-g(y)|^{q}}{|x-y|^{n+sq}}\,dx\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}\int_{Q_{3R}}\dfrac{|w(x)-w(y)|^{q}}{|x-y|^{n+sq}}\,dx\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|Q_{3R}|R^{(\theta-s)q}\|u-g\|^{(1-\theta)(q-1)}_{L^{\infty}(Q_{3R})}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|Du-Dg|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta(q-1)}{p}}\|w\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{3R})}^{1-\theta}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|Dw|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}

\displaystyle\quad+|Q_{3R}|R^{(\theta-s)q}\|g\|^{(1-\theta)(q-1)}_{L^{\infty}(Q_{3R})}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta(q-1)}{p}}\|w\|_{L^{\infty}(Q_{3R})}^{1-\theta}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}|Dw|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\theta}{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|Q_{3R}|R^{(\theta-s)q}\left[\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{3R}}}|Du|^{p}\,dx+\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptQ_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{Q_{3R}}}(|Dg|^{p}+1)\,dx\right]

with the implicit constant
c=c(\texttt{data}_{2})
.
As for
J_{2}
, noting

\displaystyle J_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{Q_{2R}}|w(x)||u(x)-(u)_{3R}|^{q-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{3R}}\dfrac{1}{|x-y|^{n+sq}}\,dy\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{Q_{2R}}|w(x)|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus Q_{3R}}\dfrac{|u(y)-(u)_{3R}|^{q-1}}{|x_{0}-y|^{n+sq}}\,dy\,dx,

we see that it resembles with (
4
). Therefore, proceeding similarly to the proof of Lemma
4.2
and additionally using (
3.7
), we conclude the proof.
∎

Let
w_{b}\in W^{1,p}_{0}(\Omega_{R})
be the weak solution of

\left\{\begin{array}[]{rlll}-\textnormal{div}(\bar{A}_{B^{\prime}_{R}}(x_{1},Dw_{b}))&=0&&\text{in }\Omega_{R},\\
w_{b}&=h_{b}&&\text{on }\partial(\Omega_{R}),\end{array}\right.

(5.6)

where

\displaystyle\bar{A}_{B^{\prime}_{R}}(x_{1},z)=\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773ptB^{\prime}_{R}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{B^{\prime}_{R}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{B^{\prime}_{R}(x_{0})}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{B^{\prime}_{R}(x_{0})}}A(x_{1},x^{\prime},z)\,dx^{\prime}.

We then have the following energy estimate:
there exists a constant
c=c(n,p,\Lambda)\geq 1
such that

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}|Dw_{b}|^{p}\,dx\leq c\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}|Dh_{b}|^{p}\,dx+c\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}|Dg|^{p}\,dx.

(5.7)

Now we proceed to the second comparison.

Lemma 5.2
.

Let
A
be
(\delta,R_{0})
-vanishing of codimension 1, for some
\delta
and
R_{0}>0
. Then for all
R<R_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}&|V(Dw_{b})-V(Dh_{b})|^{2}\,dx\leq c\;\delta^{\frac{\sigma}{p^{\prime}(1+\sigma)}}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{2R}}}|Du|^{p(1+\sigma)}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\sigma}}+\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}|Dg|^{p(1+\sigma)}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\sigma}}\right],\end{split}

where
\sigma\in(0,1)
is as in (
5.3
) and
c=c(\texttt{data}_{2})
.

Proof.

Test
h_{b}-w_{b}\in W^{1,p}_{0}(\Omega_{R})
to both (
5.1
) and (
5.6
) to have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega_{R}}\left(A(x,Dh_{b})-\bar{A}_{B^{\prime}_{R}}(x_{1},Dw_{b})\right)\cdot(Dh_{b}-Dw_{b})\,dx=0.

Then we obtain

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}

\displaystyle|V(Dw_{b})-V(Dh_{b})|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}\left(\bar{A}_{B^{\prime}_{R}}(x_{1},Dh_{b})-\bar{A}_{B^{\prime}_{R}}(x_{1},Dw_{b})\right)\cdot(Dh_{b}-Dw_{b})\,dx

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}\left(\bar{A}_{B^{\prime}_{R}}(x_{1},Dh_{b})-A(x,Dh_{b})\right)\cdot(Dh_{b}-Dw_{b})\,dx

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}\left|\bar{A}_{B^{\prime}_{R}}(x_{1},Dh_{b})-A(x,Dh_{b})\right|^{p^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}}\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}\left|Dh_{b}-Dw_{b}\right|^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p}},

(5.8)

where in the last line we have used Hölder’s inequality.
For the first term of the right-hand side of (
5.1
), we compute

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}\left|\bar{A}_{B^{\prime}_{R}}(x_{1},Dh_{b})-A(x,Dh_{b})\right|^{p^{\prime}}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}\kappa(A,Q_{R})^{p^{\prime}}\left|Dh_{b}\right|^{p}\,dx\lesssim\underbrace{\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}\kappa(A,Q_{R})^{\frac{p^{\prime}(1+\sigma)}{\sigma}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\sigma}{1+\sigma}}}_{=:I_{1}}\underbrace{\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}\left|Dh_{b}\right|^{p(1+\sigma)}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\sigma}}}_{=:I_{2}}.\end{split}

(5.9)

For
I_{1}
, we use (
1.13
) and Definition
1.5
to obtain

\displaystyle I_{1}\lesssim\left(\mathchoice{\mathop{\kern 1.99997pt\vrule width=6.00006pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.49997pt\kern-8.00003pt\intop}\nolimits_{\kern-3.27773pt\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}{\mathop{\kern 1.00006pt\vrule width=5.0pt,height=3.0pt,depth=-2.59996pt\kern-6.00006pt\intop}\nolimits_{\Omega_{R}}}\kappa(A,Q_{R})^{\frac{p^{\prime}(1+\sigma)}{\sigma}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\sigma}{1+\sigma}}\lesssim\Lambda^{\left(\frac{p^{\prime}(1+\sigma)}{\sigma}-1\right)\frac{\sigma}{1+\sigma}}\delta^{\frac{\sigma}{1+\sigma}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{\sigma}{1+\sigma}}.

(5.10)

Also, for
I_{2}
, (
5.2
), (
5.7
) and (
